[Metadata]
Item ID: ATTRIBUTE_SHARD
Shard Item ID: BEZAL
Tooltip: Blazing Resistance I Grants +4  Defense against  Infernal mobs.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; COMMON COMBAT SHARD (ID C30)
The Bezal Shard is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Blazing Resistance]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Bezal Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Bezal]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Huntraps
A Bezal Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Stronghold]], specifically in the lower room where [[Bezal|Bezals]] spawn.
### Kuudra
A Bezal Shard can be obtained from [[Kuudra|Kuudra Loot]].
### Fusion
A Bezal Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Bezal Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Blazing Resistance]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Blazing Resistance ([[Attributes]]); Grants +4–40 [[Defense]] against Infernal ([[Infernal (Mob Type)]]) mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|3|4|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|5|9|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|6|15|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|7|22|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|8|30|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|10|40|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|14|54|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|18|72|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|24|96|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Bezal Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Bezal Shard has the following description:
- This unusual blaze confounds its enemies with its disorienting appearance, attacking from unexpected angles.

## Obtaining
### Mirrorverse
The Big Brain Talisman can be claimed after completing the optional section of the fifth puzzle in the [[Mirrorverse]].
## Usage
Accessory:
Big Brain Talisman

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET_ITEM_BIG_TEETH_COMMON
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Grants +5 Crit Chance.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; COMMON PET ITEM
Big Teeth is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] that grants the [[Pets|Pet]] +5 [[Crit Chance]]. It can be purchased from [[Zog]] in the [[Village]] for 750,000 [[Coins]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
Big Teeth can be purchased from [[Zog]] in the [[Village]] for 750,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Big Teeth will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it +5 [[Crit Chance]]. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Big Teeth for 25,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Bigger Teeth]]|None|1x [[Zog Anvil]] [[Big Teeth]] 1x Big Teeth 18x [[Enchanted Gold Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Gold Block]] x6 ×3 + Big Teeth + [[Zog Anvil]] → [[Bigger Teeth]]|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|May 5, 2020|0.7.8|Added Big Teeth.|

Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes

[Shop]
Merchants: Bingo ([[Bingo (NPC)]])
Buy: 100 Bingo Points ([[Bingo]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BINGO_TALISMAN
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +3; B.I.N.G.O.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; COMMON ACCESSORY
Bingo Talisman is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]], purchasable from [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo]].
## Obtaining
Bingo Talisman can be purchased from [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo Shop]] for 100 [[Bingo|Bingo Points]] once the player has reached Ⓑ Bingo Rank I (1).
## Usage
[[Bingo Ring]] can be bought from [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo Shop]] with 1x [[Bingo Talisman]] Bingo Talisman and 150 [[Bingo|Bingo Points]] once the player has reached Ⓑ Bingo Rank II (2).
## Trivia
- It is one of the three accessories in the game that grants no buff, alongside the [[Hegemony Artifact]] and [[Hocus-Pocus Cipher]] (outside of the rift). It only grants buffs via [[Accessory Power]]
- The lore of the item is a reference to the children's song BINGO, where the letters of the word are sung to spell out the name of a farmer's dog.
  - Furthermore, when playing the game Bingo, columns are often distinguished by those letters as opposed to A, B, C...
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 29, 2021|Added Bingo Talisman.|
|Dec 1, 2021|Bingo Talisman becomes obtainable.|
|Jan 28, 2022|Bingo Talisman is no longer Co-op Soulbound.|

# Biome Stick
Aliases: Biome, Biome stick, Birch Forest Biome Stick, Deep Ocean Biome Stick, Desert Biome Stick, Dessert Biome Stick, End Biome Stick, Forest Biome Stick, Jungle Biome Stick, Mesa Biome Stick, Mushroom Biome Stick, Nether Biome Stick, Roofed Forest Biome Stick, Savanna Biome Stick, Taiga Biome Stick
Categories: Common items, Items, More info needed, Tools, Other Tools, Mechanics
[Biome Stick]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BIRCH_FOREST_BIOME_STICK; DEEP_OCEAN_BIOME_STICK; DESERT_BIOME_STICK; END_BIOME_STICK; FOREST_BIOME_STICK; JUNGLE_BIOME_STICK; NETHER_BIOME_STICK; ROOFED_FOREST_BIOME_STICK; SAVANA_BIOME_STICK; TAIGA_BIOME_STICK; MUSHROOM_BIOME_STICK; MESA_BIOME_STICK
Tooltip: Allows you to change the biome at the targeted block on your private island to Birch Forest; Right-click to use or Left-click to change the radius it effects.; Selected Radius: 3; COMMON
Biome Sticks are [[Rarity|COMMON]] Items that can be used on the player's [[Private Island]] to change its biome. Changing the biome of the island can cause a variety of changes which are mostly minor (ex: foliage color), but can also cause more major changes, like weather or mob spawning. Using the Biome Stick, the player can limit what mobs can spawn by changing the biome, although it cannot completely stop mob spawning.
The player can reset their island biome to plains by talking to [[Jerry]].
## Uses
Biome Sticks can be used in two ways:
- Specific Area: Converts a small area around where the Biome Stick is used to the new biome. The effect radius can be changed to a number between 1 and 5 (left click) or applied to a targeted block (right-click).
- Island: Talking to [[Jerry]] will let the player change the biome for their entire [[Private Island]].

### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Birries Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Yummy]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Yummy ([[Attributes]]); Grants a +10%–100% chance to drop double Berries and Sea Lumies.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

## Obtaining
The player can purchase a Bitter Iced Tea for 1,200 [[Coins]] from the [[Bartender]].
## Usage
Bitter Iced Tea buffs the [[Mana]] and [[Experience]] values of [[Potions]] with that stat by +20%. It can be used in place of an [[Awkward Potion]] in a [[Brewing Stand]] to take effect.

# Black Diamond
Aliases: Fruitful
Categories: Common items, Reforge stones
[Black Diamond]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Reforge: Fruitful
Applied To: Mining Tools ([[Tools]])
Apply Cost: 5,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining II (2) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Mining Merchant]]
Buy: 100 [[Coins]]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ONYX
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Fruitful reforge when combined with a Mining Tool.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Mining Speed  Mining Fortune; Fruitful Bonus Grants +75 Defense while on  Mining Islands.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 2.; COMMON REFORGE STONE
The Black Diamond is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Fruitful]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]]. It grants [[Mining Speed]], and [[Mining Fortune]].
## Obtaining
The Black Diamond can be purchased from the [[Mining Merchant]] in [[Hub Island|The Hub]] for 100 [[Coins]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Black Diamond can be applied to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Fruitful]] reforge, to grant [[Mining Speed]] and [[Mining Fortune]], which scale with the Mining Tool's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining II (2)]]. A Mining Tool that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Fruitful]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Fruitful Bonus, which grants 75 [[Defense]] on [[Mining Islands]].
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+2 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]]) +5 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+4 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]]) +10 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+6 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]]) +15 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+8 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]]) +25 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+10 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]]) +40 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+12 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]]) +55 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+15 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]]) +70 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Fruitful Bonus; Grants +75 Defense while on Mining Islands.|Fruitful Bonus; Grants +75 Defense while on Mining Islands.|Fruitful Bonus; Grants +75 Defense while on Mining Islands.|Fruitful Bonus; Grants +75 Defense while on Mining Islands.|Fruitful Bonus; Grants +75 Defense while on Mining Islands.|Fruitful Bonus; Grants +75 Defense while on Mining Islands.|Fruitful Bonus; Grants +75 Defense while on Mining Islands.|
|Cost to apply|5,000 [[Coins]]|5,000 [[Coins]]|5,000 [[Coins]]|5,000 [[Coins]]|5,000 [[Coins]]|5,000 [[Coins]]|5,000 [[Coins]]|

The Bunbun Shard is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Chocolate Speed]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Bunbun Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Bunbun]] with a [[Lassos|Lasso]].
### Huntraps
A Bunbun Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Torrhus Canyon]] (On Land).
### Fusion
A Bunbun Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Bunbun Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Chocolate Speed]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Chocolate Speed ([[Attributes]]); Increases your [[Chocolate Factory]] speed by +0.25%–2.5%.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

Tooltip: Accessory Power: +3; Gain +2 Health Regen while burning.; Trial of Fire II &quot;Fire guy!&quot;; * Soulbound * Works while in Accessory Bag!; COMMON ACCESSORY
The Campfire Badge is a [[Accessories|Progressive Accessory]] acquired and upgraded by completing the [[Trials of Fire|Trial of Fire]]. It has a total of five tiers, from its [[Rarity|COMMON]] tier to its [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] tier.
It heals the player every second while they are on fire, and once the badge's tier has reached its [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] tier, it will heal the player even while they aren't on fire.
The badge is first obtained from [[Ryan]] as a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] after surviving the first level of the [[Trials of Fire|Trial of Fire]]. Completing more stages of the [[Trials of Fire|Trial of Fire]] will raise the badge's rarity sequentially until it reaches [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]].
Note that this item can only be obtained once, but if it is lost you can visit [[Rusty]] in the [[Gold Mine]] and get it back for a fee.

## Trivia
- The description (Fire guy!) is a reference from the popular TV show The Office (U.S.).
- Every trial after 22 doesn't give a rarity upgrade, only regeneration stats.
- The price of this item at [[Rusty]] is not affected by the [[Shady Ring]] and its upgraded versions.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CARROT_BAIT
Tooltip: Fishing Bait Consumes on Cast; Chance of fishing up... a rabbit?; COMMON BAIT
Carrot Bait is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Bait]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Carrot III (3) ([[Carrot]])|1x [[Carrot]] 1x [[Raw Cod]]|Recipe: [[Carrot]] + [[Raw Cod]] → Carrot Bait|
## Usage
### Fishing
Carrot Bait can be kept in either the player's inventory or [[Bait Sack]]. When casting a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], a Carrot Bait will be consumed to grant a 5% (1 in 20) chance to fish up a [[Carrot King]]. The effects last until the player reels in their rod. If the player doesn't catch anything when reeling, or otherwise cancels their catch, the Bait will be refunded.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Poignant Carrot Bait]]|None|[[Carrot Bait]] 1x Carrot Bait 32x [[Carrot Zest]]|Recipe: [[Carrot Zest]] x4 ×8 + Carrot Bait → [[Poignant Carrot Bait]]|

The Cavernfish Shard is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Cave Fishing]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Cavernfish Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Cavernfish]] with a [[Critter Capsule]] in the [[Critter Safari]].
### Fusion
A Cavernfish Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Cavernfish Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Cave Fishing]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Cave Fishing ([[Attributes]]); Grants +3–30 [[Fishing Speed]] while on Mining Islands.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

Tooltip: Can be placed in the Fossil Excavator alongside a Suspicious Scrap to begin the excavation process!; Charges: 16; COMMON CHISEL
The Chisel is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Chisels|Chisel]].
## Obtaining
A Chisel can be bought from [[Researcher Beryl]] at [[Fossil Research Center]] with 5 [[Fossil Essence]]. A Chisel is also given by interacting with [[Dr. Stone]] for the first time.
## Usage
A Chisel can be used in the [[Fossil Excavator]] with [[Suspicious Scrap|Suspicious Scraps]] to find [[Fossils]] and other loot.
### Forging
The Chisel, along with a variety of other items, can be used to forge a [[Reinforced Chisel]] once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VIII (8)]]. It takes 30 seconds to forge.
#### Forging Tree
- 1 [[Reinforced Chisel]]
  - [[Chisel]] 1 Chisel 5 [[Fossil Essence]]
  - 2 [[Refined Tungsten]] 320 (5 stacks) [[Enchanted Tungsten]] 51,200 (800 stacks) [[Tungsten]]
  - 2 [[Refined Umber]] 320 [[Enchanted Umber]] 51,200 [[Umber]]
  - 1 [[Bejeweled Handle]] 3 [[Glacite Jewel]]

# Choconut
Categories: Common items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mutations
[Choconut]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

Auctionable: ✕ No

Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CHOCONUT
Tooltip: Its shell is tough, but inside, it's filled with pure chocolate.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; COMMON MUTATION
A Choconut is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Choconut can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 2 [[Cocoa Beans]] within 1 block of farmland.
When placed near another crop, Choconut provides immunity to negative effects.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Choconuts also drop 200 (3 stacks plus 8) [[Cocoa Beans]] and +400 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Choconut can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 10,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 5 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Choconut grants an additional 250 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Choconuts are used to create a [[Chocoberry]], [[Creambloom]], and [[Turtlellini]].
## Gallery
- Choconut in the Greenhouse Choconut in the [[Greenhouse]]

# Chum Bucket
Aliases: Bucket of Chum, Bucket of chum, Empty Chum Bucket, Full Chum Bucket
Categories: Common items, Uncommon items, Items, Craftable items, Fishing
[Chum Bucket]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Empty Chumcap Bucket]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Moby]]
Buy: 8x Glowing Mushrooms ([[Glowing Mushroom]]) 2,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: EMPTY_CHUM_BUCKET
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable: ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum: ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FULL_CHUM_BUCKET
Tooltip: Place it down next to water.; Sea Creatures slain nearby will drop +1 Chum.; Up to +4 total from buckets!; COMMON
The Empty Chum Bucket is an item that can be placed down and filled with [[Chum]]. Full Chum Buckets can be used to buy items from [[Moby]].
## Obtaining
An Empty Chum Bucket can be bought from [[Moby]] for 8 [[Glowing Mushroom|Glowing Mushrooms]] and 2,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
### Fishing
Empty Chum Buckets can be placed next to [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]]. While any player's Chum Bucket is placed nearby, [[Sea Creatures]] killed by the player will drop 1 [[Chum]]. The amount of Chum dropped is increased by 1 per nearby Chum Bucket, up to 4.
The player can right click on their or another player's Chum Bucket to deposit their Chum. They will gain 20 [[Coins]] and +10 [[Fishing|Fishing XP]] per Chum, which can be increased by the [[Luminous Bracelet]] if the Chum is deposited into another player's Chum Bucket. Once the player's Chum Bucket has 10 Chum in it, it can be picked up, and will become a Full Chum Bucket.
If the player walks too far away from their placed Chum Bucket or warps to a different server, it will be automatically picked up, causing it to lose all Chum inside of it, even if it was full.
### [[Moby]]
Full Chum Buckets can be used to buy items from [[Moby]].
### Crafting
A Full Chum Bucket can be alternatively obtained by crafting.
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x Empty Chum Bucket ([[Chum Bucket]]) 10x [[Chum]]|Recipe: [[Chum]] x10 + [[Chum Bucket]] → [[Chum Bucket]]|
|None|1x [[Empty Chumcap Bucket]] 10x [[Chum]]|Recipe: [[Chum]] x10 + [[Empty Chumcap Bucket]] → [[Chum Bucket]]|
The Empty Chum Bucket can be upgraded to an [[Empty Chumcap Bucket]].
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x Empty Chum Bucket ([[Chum Bucket]]) 8x [[Agaricus Chumcap]]|Recipe: [[Agaricus Chumcap]] ×8 + [[Chum Bucket]] → [[Empty Chumcap Bucket]]|
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Placing|You placed down a Chum Bucket!|
|Right-clicking bucket|Deposit Chums in this bucket!|
|Right-clicking someone else's bucket|Deposit Chums in this bucket! Get your own bucket in the Glowing Mushroom Cave, in The Farming Islands.|
|Depositing [[Chum]]|You deposited # Chums in your own bucket!|
|Depositing [[Chum]] in someone else's bucket|You deposited # Chums in <player>'s bucket!|
|Other player depositing [[Chum]]|<player> deposited # Chums in your bucket!|
|Picking up full bucket|You collected your filled Chum Bucket!|
|Too far away from bucket|Automatically picked up the Chum Bucket you left back there!|
|Placing second bucket|You already have a bucket placed out there. Fill that one first!|
## Trivia
The Chum Bucket is a reference to the TV show Spongebob Squarepants, where Plankton owns a restaurant called the Chum Bucket.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Sep 6, 2022|Added Empty Chum Bucket.|
|Sep 6, 2022|Added Full Chum Bucket.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Chum Buckets to the [[Bazaar]].|
|Jul 18, 2023|Fixed not being able to fill Chum Buckets from [[Ironman]] players when on the same [[Co-op]].|
|Aug 1, 2023|0.19.2|Fixed buckets being placeable at the highest build level.|
|Aug 6, 2024|0.20.5|Full Chum Buckets can now be picked up without an empty hand.|
|Aug 6, 2024|0.20.5|Full Chum Buckets can now be crafted.|

Item ID: ATTRIBUTE_SHARD
Shard Item ID: COD
Tooltip: Cheapstake I Gain +1% more Coins from fishing treasures.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; COMMON WATER SHARD (ID C5)
The Cod Shard is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Cheapstake]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Cod Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Cod]] with a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]].
### Huntraps
A Cod Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Murkwater Depths]] (Underwater) or [[Murkwater Shallows]] (Underwater).
### Fusion
A Cod Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Cod Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Cheapstake]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Cheapstake ([[Attributes]]); Gain +1%–10% more [[Coins]] from fishing treasures.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|3|4|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|5|9|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|6|15|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|7|22|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|8|30|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|10|40|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|14|54|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|18|72|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|24|96|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Cod Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||

When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Cod Shard has the following description:
- Their streamlined bodies allow them to make quick, agile turns, escaping threats with ease.

Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Gives +20% pet exp for Combat.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; COMMON PET ITEM
A Combat Exp Boost is a [[Pet Items|Pet Item]].
## Obtaining
### Common
A [[Rarity|COMMON]] Combat Exp Boost can be purchased from [[Zog]] in the [[Village]] for 60,000 [[Coins]].
### Uncommon
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|

### Rare

|[Lv44]|[[Obsidian Defender]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.01%|
### Epic
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv44]|[[Obsidian Defender]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.0033%|
|[Lv55]|[[Seer]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.002%|
## Usage
|Rarity|Usage|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Right-clicking a Pet ([[Pets]]) whilst holding a Combat Exp Boost will equip it to the Pet ([[Pets]]), granting it +20% Pet ([[Pets]]) Exp for [[Combat]]. If another Pet Item ([[Pet Items]]) were equipped on the Pet ([[Pets]]), it would refund the Combat Exp Boost for 25,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Right-clicking a Pet ([[Pets]]) whilst holding a Combat Exp Boost will equip it to the Pet ([[Pets]]), granting it +30% Pet ([[Pets]]) Exp for [[Combat]]. If another Pet Item ([[Pet Items]]) were equipped on the Pet ([[Pets]]), it would refund the Combat Exp Boost for 50,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Right-clicking a Pet ([[Pets]]) whilst holding a Combat Exp Boost will equip it to the Pet ([[Pets]]), granting it +40% Pet ([[Pets]]) Exp for [[Combat]]. If another Pet Item ([[Pet Items]]) were equipped on the Pet ([[Pets]]), it would refund the Combat Exp Boost for 100,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Right-clicking a Pet ([[Pets]]) whilst holding a Combat Exp Boost will equip it to the Pet ([[Pets]]), granting it +50% Pet ([[Pets]]) Exp for [[Combat]]. If another Pet Item ([[Pet Items]]) were equipped on the Pet ([[Pets]]), it would refund the Combat Exp Boost for 250,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.|

# Common Fighting Booster
Aliases: Fighting Booster
Categories: Common items, Items
[Common Fighting Booster]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FIGHTING_BOOSTER
Tooltip: Booster Combinable in Anvil; Applies the ४ Fighting booster when combined with Foraging Armor, Equipment, or Axes.; Increases damage against Foraging Island mobs.; COMMON BOOSTER
A Common Fighting Booster is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Boosters|Booster]]. It grants additional damage to [[Foraging]] gear.
## Obtaining
A Common Fighting Booster can be purchased from [[Agatha]] for 100 (1 stack plus 36) [[Agatha's Coupon]] at Agatha Milestone IV.
## Usage
A Common Fighting Booster can be applied to [[Foraging]] gear in an [[Anvil]] to deal additional damage to mobs on [[Foraging]] creatures. Only one can be applied per piece. The damage gained depends on type:
- [[Equipment]] gains 1%
- [[Armor]] gains 2%
- [[Axes]] gain 5%
Common Fighting Booster is also has to applied to the item to be upgraded to [[Uncommon Fighting Booster Upgrade|Uncommon Fighting Booster]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 5, 2025|Added Fighting Booster.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Added Fighting Booster.|
|Aug 4, 2026|0.27|Renamed to Common Fighting Booster.|
|Aug 4, 2026|0.27|Changed amount of [[Damage]] granted from 2%/3%/5% to 1%/2%/5% on [[Equipment]], [[Armor]] and [[Axes]] respectively.|

A Common Foraging Fortune Booster is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Boosters|Booster]]. It grants [[Foraging Fortune]] to [[Foraging]] gear.
## Obtaining
A Common Foraging Fortune Booster can be bought from [[Sawyer]] for 50,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
A Common Foraging Fortune Booster can be applied to [[Foraging]] gear in an [[Anvil]] to gain [[Foraging Fortune]]. Only one can be applied per piece. The [[Foraging Fortune]] gained depends on type:
- [[Equipment]] gains 2 [[Foraging Fortune]]
- [[Armor]] gains 5 [[Foraging Fortune]]
- [[Axes]] gain 10 [[Foraging Fortune]]
A Common Foraging Fortune Booster has to be applied to an item in order to be able to upgrade to an [[Uncommon Foraging Fortune Booster Upgrade|Uncommon Foraging Fortune Booster]].

Item ID: FORAGING_WISDOM_BOOSTER
Tooltip: Booster Combinable in Anvil; Applies the ⸙ Foraging Wisdom booster when combined with Foraging Armor, Equipment, or Axes.; Grants ☯ Foraging Wisdom.; COMMON BOOSTER
A Common Foraging Wisdom Booster is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Boosters|Booster]]. It grants [[Foraging Wisdom]] to [[Foraging]] gear.
## Obtaining
A Common Foraging Wisdom Booster can be obtained from [[Tree Gifts]] with a 0.5% from fig trees and 0.8% from mangrove trees.
## Usage
A Common Foraging Wisdom Booster can be applied to [[Foraging]] gear in an [[Anvil]] to gain [[Foraging Wisdom]]. Only one can be applied per piece. The [[Foraging Wisdom]] gained depends on type:
- [[Equipment]] gains 0.5 [[Foraging Wisdom]]
- [[Armor]] gains 1 [[Foraging Wisdom]]
- [[Axes]] gain 2 [[Foraging Wisdom]]
A Common Foraging Wisdom Booster has to be applied to an item in order to be able to upgrade to an [[Uncommon Foraging Wisdom Booster Upgrade|Uncommon Foraging Wisdom Booster]].
## History

The Coralot Shard is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Bucket Lover]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Coralot Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Coralot]] with a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]] or a [[Lassos|Lasso]].
### Huntraps
A Coralot Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Murkwater Depths]] (Underwater).
### Fusion
A Coralot Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Coralot Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Bucket Lover]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Bucket Lover ([[Attributes]]); You have a +5%–50% chance to gain extra Chums ([[Chum]]).
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|3|4|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|5|9|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|6|15|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|7|22|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|8|30|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|10|40|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|14|54|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|18|72|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|24|96|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Coralot Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

# Corrupted Bait
Categories: Common items, Items in Sacks, Items, Craftable items, Fishing
[Corrupted Bait]
Type: [[Bait]]
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]); Medium Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]); Large Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]])
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CORRUPTED_BAIT
Tooltip: Fishing Bait Consumes on Cast; Decreases your  Fishing Speed by 50%, but lures a special Trophy Fish in the Crimson Isle.; COMMON BAIT
Corrupted Bait is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Bait]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Magmafish V (5) ([[Magmafish]])|1x [[Corrupted Fragment]] 1x [[Magmafish]]|Recipe: [[Corrupted Fragment]] + [[Magmafish]] → Corrupted Bait x16|
## Usage
### Fishing
Corrupted Bait can be kept in either the player's inventory or [[Bait Sack]]. When casting a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], a Corrupted Bait will be consumed to reduce the player's [[Fishing Speed]] by -50% and grant a chance to catch an [[Trophy Fish|Obfuscated-1]] [[Trophy Fish]] while fishing in Lava on the [[Crimson Isle]]. The effects last until the player reels in their rod. If the player doesn't catch anything when reeling, or otherwise cancels their catch, the Bait will be refunded.
## Tips
- Since [[Trophy Fish|Obfuscated-1]] can also be obtained from [[Corrupted Mobs|Corrupted]] [[Sea Creatures]], which can be created using [[Totem of Corruption]], it's better to avoid using this bait.
## Trivia
- It is the only Bait to make a sound when used. In this case, it makes a lower-pitched screaming [[Enderman]] sound.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 12, 2025|Corrupted Bait can no longer be caught as treasure.|
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Corrupted Bait as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

|Aug 18, 2022|0.14|Reworded the Corrupted Bait's lore.|

|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Corrupted Bait as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|

|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv54]|[[Rider of the Deep]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
## Usage
### Fishing
Dark Bait can be kept in either the player's inventory or [[Bait Sack]]. When casting a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], a Dark Bait will be consumed. At night it will grant a 25% increase in weight for rare [[Sea Creatures]] (those with a weight below 400). It has no effect during the day. If the player doesn't catch anything when reeling, or otherwise cancels their catch, the Bait will be refunded.

# Dedication
Aliases: DEDICATION;4, Dedication Enchantment
Categories: Common items, Enchantments
[Dedication]
Type: Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Enchantment Requirements]
Minimum Level: I
Maximum Level: IV
Applied To: Farming Tools
Req. [[Enchanting]] Level: Enchanting XII (12) ([[Enchanting]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_DEDICATION
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Dedication I Grants 0.5 Farming Fortune for crops, multiplied by your Garden milestone for that crop.; Applicable on: Farming Tool Apply Cost: 20 Exp Levels; Requires Enchanting 12 to apply.; COMMON
Dedication is a farming tool [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that grants [[Crop Fortune]], multiplied by your [[The Garden|Crop Milestone]] tier of the corresponding crop. It can be applied to [[Farming Tools]], starting at [[Enchanting|Enchanting XII (12)]].
## Obtaining
The Dedication [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained in various ways:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Dedication I|Purchased from SkyMart ([[The Garden]]) in [[The Garden]] for 250 [[Copper]]|
|Dedication IV|0.5% (1 in 200) chance from [[Librarian]] Visitor ([[Garden Visitors]])|
|Dedication IV|Guaranteed from [[Ravenous Rhino]] Visitor ([[Garden Visitors]])|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Dedication) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Dedication III.
### Bazaar
The Dedication [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Dedication|I|
|Dedication|II|
|Dedication|III|
|Dedication|IV|
## Ability
It grants up to 2 [[Crop Fortune]] per [[The Garden|Crop Milestone]] tier of the corresponding crop, up to 92 [[Crop Fortune]] at tier 46.
|Tier|Per milestone tier|At max milestone (46)|
|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ|+0.5 [[Crop Fortune]]|+23 [[Crop Fortune]]|
|Ⅱ|+0.75 [[Crop Fortune]]|+34.5 [[Crop Fortune]]|
|Ⅲ|+1 [[Crop Fortune]]|+46 [[Crop Fortune]]|
|Ⅳ|+2 [[Crop Fortune]]|+92 [[Crop Fortune]]|
## Applying
The Dedication [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be applied to [[Farming Tools]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XII (12)]] to apply.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|20 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅱ|25 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅲ|30 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅳ|100 [[Experience]]|
## Trivia
- Despite the tooltip stating [[Farming Fortune]], it actually provides [[Crop Fortune]].
## Tips
- The chance for a Dedication IV book can be increased by creating a new profile and ensuring the only [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] visitor unlocked is the [[Librarian]].
  - Currently this strategy is not as effective, because 12 [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Hub Villagers|Villagers]] are unlocked automatically.
  - Most visitors are unlocked by simply talking to them, so care must be taken to avoid talking to undesired visitors. However, up to 5 [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] visitors can be unlocked without reducing the player's chances significantly, as 5 visitors will queue up at a time anyway.
  - There is no need to avoid unlocking visitors of other rarities, as the rarity is always rolled first, and if no visitor of the rolled rarity is unlocked, the player will simply not get any new visitor instead.
  - It should be noted that doing this with the intent of transferring to another profile is considered boosting and is against Hypixel rules. The only exception to this is doing it on a [[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]] profile, which is permitted because it explicitly provides a way to transfer items to another profile.
  - This strategy is commonly used by players on [[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]] profiles, either to obtain Dedication IV books for their [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] profiles, or as a money-making method.
- Another method for obtaining Dedication IV is, if the player has served at least 50 unique visitors and [[Finnegan]]'s Blooming Business perk is active, to keep all other [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] visitors in the queue. This guarantees that the next [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] visitor to appear will be [[Ravenous Rhino]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 23, 2023|Added Dedication I–IV.|
|Mar 31, 2026|Dedication can now be applied to [[Farming Tools]], instead of [[Axes]] and [[Hoes]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Mar 28, 2023|0.18.2|Added Dedication I–IV.|
|Jul 2, 2024|0.20.3|Dedication IV can now also be obtained from [[Ravenous Rhino]].|
|Apr 28, 2026|0.24.4|Dedication can now be applied to [[Farming Tools]], instead of [[Axes]] and [[Hoes]].|

# Desert Island

Tooltip: Place this in your private world to spawn a Desert island!; COMMON
Desert Island is a [[Spawnable Islands|Spawnable Island]] unlocked at [[Cactus|Cactus V (5)]]. It spawns a desert island on use.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Cactus V (5) ([[Cactus]])|5x [[Sugar Cane]] 10x [[Cactus]] 30x [[Sand]]|Recipe: [[Cactus]] x5 ×2 + [[Sugar Cane]] x5 + [[Sand]] x5 ×6 → Desert Island|
## Usage
### Spawn Island
Placing the item on the [[Private Island]] will spawn a Desert Island 16 blocks away in front of the player.
It is one of only four ways to start the [[Sand]] collection with the others being the [[Pond Island]], buying a [[Sand Minion]], or the desert settlement.
### Spawn Crystal
Spawning one is currently the only known way of obtaining the [[Desert Crystal]], which has an effective radius of 16.

|[[Smooth Sandstone]]|Smooth Sandstone|5x|
|[[Cactus]]|[[Cactus]]|17x|
|[[Sugar Cane]]|[[Sugar Cane]]|11x|
|[[Desert Crystal]]|[[Desert Crystal]]|1x|
## Trivia
- Before the Mushroom Desert Revamp update this island was only source of the Sand collection.
- As soon as island is placed, 3 [[Sand]] blocks will immediately fall off.

[Item Properties]

Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No

[Metadata]
Item ID: ATTRIBUTE_SHARD
Shard Item ID: DUMPSTER_DIVER
Tooltip: Aquatic Resistance I Grants +4  Defense against  Aquatic mobs.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; COMMON WATER SHARD (ID C8)
The Dumpster Diver Shard is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Aquatic Resistance]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Dumpster Diver Shard can be obtained from killing a [[Dumpster Diver]] with enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Huntraps
A Dumpster Diver Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Backwater Bayou]] (Underwater).
### Fusion
A Dumpster Diver Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Dumpster Diver Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Aquatic Resistance]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Aquatic Resistance ([[Attributes]]); Grants +4–40 [[Defense]] against [[Aquatic]] mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|3|4|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|5|9|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|6|15|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|7|22|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|8|30|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|10|40|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|14|54|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|18|72|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|24|96|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Dumpster Diver Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||

[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Dumpster Diver Shard has the following description:
- What else is the ocean if not the largest, deepest, most bountiful dumpster of them all?

# Dustgrain
Categories: Common items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients, Mutations
[Dustgrain]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DUSTGRAIN
Tooltip: Fragile and dry, it crumbles with the slightest touch.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; COMMON MUTATION
A Dustgrain is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Dustgrain can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 2 [[Wheat]] within 1 block of farmland.
When placed near another crop, Dustgrain increases yield by 20%.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Dustgrains also drop 100 (1 stack plus 36) [[Wheat]] and +400 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Dustgrain can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 10,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 5 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Dustgrain grants an additional 250 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Dustgrain are used to create a [[Duskbloom]] and [[Snoozling]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])|DNA Analysis Milestone I (1) ([[Crop Analyzer]])|1x [[Enchanted Leather]] [[Dustgrain]] 4x Dustgrain 4x [[Veilshroom]]|Recipe: [[Veilshroom]] ×4 + Dustgrain ×4 + [[Enchanted Leather]] → [[Mutations Sack]]|
## Gallery
- Dustgrain in the Greenhouse Dustgrain in the [[Greenhouse]]
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Dustgrain.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Dustgrain.|
|Feb 2, 2026|Reduced Dustgrain growing requirements to 2 [[Wheat]].|

# Dwarven Lantern
Categories: Common items, Items, Tools, Mining, Support Items
[Dwarven Lantern]
Type: Support Item ([[Support Items]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Upgrades]

[Item Properties]

Item ID: LANTERN
Tooltip: Mining Deployable; Ability: Deploy RIGHT CLICK Place an orb for 5m buffing up to 5; players within 30 blocks.; Costs 50% of max mana.; Orb Buff: Dwarven Lantern ▪ Grants +20 Mining Speed; Only one deployable buff applies.; COMMON DEPLOYABLE
A Dwarven Lantern is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Deployables|Deployable]].
## Obtaining
### NPCs
A Dwarven Lantern can be purchased from [[Lumina]] for 10,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Using the ability of a Dwarven Lantern costs 50% of player's max [[Mana]], this can be reduced down to 40% using [[Mana Disintegrator|Mana Disintegrators]]. Casting the ability grants up to 5 players in 30 block radius the following stats:
- +20 [[Mining Speed]]
When used alongside multiple or different lantern deployables, only one deployable effect applies and only the one of higher rarity will apply its effects.

# Egg Hunt
Categories: Common items, Items, Social
[Egg Hunt]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Skill Level ([[Skills]]) Requirement: Social III (3) ([[Social]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Amelia]]
Buy: 1,000 [[Coins]] (For 2)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: EGG_HUNT
Tooltip: Place an egg which can be found on your island by guests for island points.; Maximum of 10.; ❣ Requires Social Skill 3.; COMMON
Egg Hunt, also referred to as Easter Egg, is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item that is placed for others to find and gain [[Island Points]].
## Obtaining
Egg Hunt can be bought from [[Amelia]]. It costs 1,000 [[Coins]] for [[Egg Hunt]] 2 Egg Hunts.
## Usage
It can be placed on the [[Private Island]] for other players to find. Up to [[Egg Hunt]] 10 Egg Hunts can be placed on an island after reaching [[Social|Social III (3)]]. Only 10 eggs are recommended to be purchased at a time due to this. Each egg found gives +10 [[Island Points]].
## Bugs
- There is a bug possible that allows for removal of egg hunts without the island recognizing it was removed. This can lead to a message being displayed saying there are far more eggs left then there actually is.
## History

|Nov 12, 2020|0.9.12|Added Egg Hunt|
|Unknown Date|Changed rarity from UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) to COMMON ([[Rarity]])|

The Egglocator is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] tool purchasable through the Chocolate Shop. It is used to locate [[Chocolate Eggs|Chocolate Rabbit Eggs]] during [[Hoppity's Hunt]].
## Obtaining
The Egglocator can be bought for 7,500,000 Chocolate in the [[Chocolate Factory|Chocolate Shop]], provided the player's [[Chocolate Factory]] is at least level 2.
## Usage
Item:
Egglocator
[Effects]
[Ability: Egglocator RIGHT CLICK; Points towards the nearest unclaimed Chocolate Rabbit Egg!; Cooldown: 5s]
When used during [[Hoppity's Hunt]], the Egglocator creates a trail of particle effects pointing in the direction of the nearest [[Chocolate Eggs|Chocolate Rabbit Egg]] in the current island that the player hasn't found yet.

# Enchanted Bone Meal
Categories: Common items, Items, More info needed, Bone Collection, Enchanted
[Enchanted Bone Meal]
Type: Enchanted item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Bone II (2) ([[Bone]])
Tooltip Text: Instantly grows crops and saplings.
Source: [[Farm Merchant]] stock [[Trades]]
Uses: Instantly fertilize plants
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Farm Merchant]] [[Windmill Operator]]
Buy: 2 [[Coins]]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Trade]
From: 32 [[Bone]]
To: 1 Enchanted Bone Meal
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTED_BONE_MEAL
Tooltip: Instantly grows crops and saplings.; COMMON
Enchanted Bone Meal is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] enchanted item unlocked at [[Bone|Bone II (2)]].
## Obtaining
### Trades
|Requirements|Trade From|Trade To|
|-|-|-|
|Bone II (2) ([[Bone]])|32 [[Bone]]|1 Enchanted Bone Meal|
### Purchase
|Merchant|Price ([[Coins]]) 1x​|Price ([[Coins]]) 5x​|Price ([[Coins]]) 10x​|Price ([[Coins]]) 32x​|Price ([[Coins]]) 64x|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Farm Merchant]]|2 [[Coins]]​|10 [[Coins]]​|20 [[Coins]]​|64 [[Coins]]​|128 [[Coins]]|
## Usage
Enchanted Bone Meal can be used as a fertilizer for instantly growing any plants on the [[Private Island]]. When used on a [[Grass]] block, it also generates new plants, similarly to [[Bone Meal]]. While this includes [[Saplings]] and [[Melon]]/[[Pumpkin]] Stems, it won't make actual [[Melon]]/[[Pumpkin]] Blocks grow or grow faster. Enchanted Bone Meal is also used in [[Pomtair]]'s quest on the [[Crimson Isle]].
Enchanted Bone Meal can also be used on a [[Dead Bush]] or [[Red Thornleaf|Red Thornleaves]] during the [[Tyashoi Alchemist|Alchemy Experiments]] Halloween Event to obtain [[Dead Seed|Dead Seeds]]. It will be consumed upon use.

|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|Lowered the cost of Enchanted Bone Meal from 64 (1 stack) [[Bone]] to 32 [[Bone]].|
|Unknown Date|Added a 640 daily buy limit to Enchanted Bone Meal at the [[Farm Merchant]] and [[Windmill Operator]].|

Enchanted Bread is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Minion Fuel]]. It boosts the speed of a minion by 5% for 12 hours when placed inside the minion.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Wheat V (5) ([[Wheat]])|60x [[Wheat]]|Recipe: [[Wheat]] x10 ×6 → Enchanted Bread|
## Usage
### Minion fuel
When used as a fuel, it increases the speed of the player's [[Minions|Minion]] by 5% for 12 hours. Enchanted Bread can be placed in all [[Minions]].
## Tips
- This fuel is outclassed by others in similar price range and should not be used. [[Enchanted Coal]] has double the duration and bonus, while only costing more.

# Enchanted Shears
Categories: Common items, Minion upgrades, Craftable items, Minion Modifiers
[Enchanted Shears]
Type: [[Shears]]
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 2 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTED_SHEARS
Tooltip: Minion Upgrade; This item can be used as a minion upgrade for sheep generators. This will automatically shear sheep for you as they spawn.; COMMON SHEARS
Enchanted Shears are a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Minion Upgrades|Minion Upgrade]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|2x [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] ×2 → Enchanted Shears|
## Usage
### Minion Upgrade
Enchanted Shears can be placed in a [[Sheep Minion]]. It automatically shears the [[Sheep]] the minion spawns. Using NPC values, this is more profitable than [[Diamond Spreading]] is on a [[Sheep Minion]]. Enchanted Shears can also be used as normal [[Shears]], to destroy [[Leaves]] faster.
Hitting a [[Fairy]] in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] whilst the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is in the [[Village]] with Enchanted Shears will give the player [[Fairy Wings]].
## Trivia
- Despite being called [[Sheep Minion|Sheep Minions]], Enchanted Shears refers to them as sheep generators, referencing their internal IDs.
- Enchanted Shears cannot be crafted with Supercraft or Quick Craft.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Oct 5, 2022|Added Enchanted Shears.|

# Farming Exp Boost
Categories: Common items, Items, Pet items
[Farming Exp Boost]
Type: Pet Item ([[Pet Items]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Zog]] (COMMON ([[Rarity]]) / RARE ([[Rarity]])); [[Hungry Hiker]] (UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])); [[Duncan]] (EPIC ([[Rarity]]))
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Zog]]
Buy: 50,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET_ITEM_FARMING_SKILL_BOOST_COMMON; PET_ITEM_FARMING_SKILL_BOOST_UNCOMMON; PET_ITEM_FARMING_SKILL_BOOST_RARE; PET_ITEM_FARMING_SKILL_BOOST_EPIC
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Gives +20% pet exp for Farming.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; COMMON PET ITEM
A Farming Exp Boost is a [[Pet Items|Pet Item]].
## Obtaining
- A [[Rarity|COMMON]] Farming Exp Boost can be purchased from [[Zog]] in the [[Village]] for 60,000 [[Coins]].
- An [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] Farming Exp Boost is rewarded from the [[Hungry Hiker]] for saving him, and is also a reward from the [[Hungry Hiker]] [[Garden Visitors|Garden Visitor]].
- A [[Rarity|RARE]] Farming Exp Boost can be purchased from [[Zog]] in the [[Village]] for 500,000 [[Coins]].
- An [[Rarity|EPIC]] Farming Exp Boost can be purchased from [[Duncan]] in the [[Catacombs]] for 1,500,000 [[Coins]], and also has a 5% (1 in 20) chance to be rewarded from accepting the offer from [[Duncan]] as a [[Garden Visitors|Garden Visitor]].
## Usage
|Rarity|Usage|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Right-clicking a Pet ([[Pets]]) whilst holding a Farming Exp Boost will equip it to the Pet ([[Pets]]), granting it +20% Pet ([[Pets]]) Exp for [[Farming]]. If another Pet Item ([[Pet Items]]) were equipped on the Pet ([[Pets]]), it would refund the Farming Exp Boost for 25,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Right-clicking a Pet ([[Pets]]) whilst holding a Farming Exp Boost will equip it to the Pet ([[Pets]]), granting it +30% Pet ([[Pets]]) Exp for [[Farming]]. If another Pet Item ([[Pet Items]]) were equipped on the Pet ([[Pets]]), it would refund the Farming Exp Boost for 50,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Right-clicking a Pet ([[Pets]]) whilst holding a Farming Exp Boost will equip it to the Pet ([[Pets]]), granting it +40% Pet ([[Pets]]) Exp for [[Farming]]. If another Pet Item ([[Pet Items]]) were equipped on the Pet ([[Pets]]), it would refund the Farming Exp Boost for 100,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Right-clicking a Pet ([[Pets]]) whilst holding a Farming Exp Boost will equip it to the Pet ([[Pets]]), granting it +50% Pet ([[Pets]]) Exp for [[Farming]]. If another Pet Item ([[Pet Items]]) were equipped on the Pet ([[Pets]]), it would refund the Farming Exp Boost for 250,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|May 5, 2020|0.7.8|Added Farming Exp Boost.|

# Farming Island
Categories: Common items, Items, Craftable items, Wheat Collection, Spawnable islands, Blocks, Farming
[Farming Island]
Type: Island Spawner
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Wheat VI (6) ([[Wheat]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 135x (2 stacks plus 7 (135 in total)) [[Wheat]] 30x [[Dirt]]
Bazaar Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from the Bazaar.): (not including: 30 Dirt)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FARMING_ISLAND
Tooltip: Place this in your private world to spawn a Farming island!; COMMON
The Farming Island is a [[Spawnable Islands|Spawnable Island]] unlocked at [[Wheat|Wheat VI (6)]].
Placing it creates an Island composed of [[Stone]], [[Granite]], [[Dirt]], [[Hardened Clay|Cyan Hardened Clay]], [[Podzol]], and [[Seeds]].
It also contains a [[Wheat Crystal]], which grows [[Wheat]] on tilled soil, with an effective radius of 6.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Wheat VI (6) ([[Wheat]])|30x [[Dirt]] 96x (1 stack plus 32 (96 in total)) [[Wheat]]|Recipe: [[Wheat]] x32 ×3 + [[Dirt]] x5 ×6 → Farming Island|
## Island Contents
A Farming Island contains the following:
- 658 (10 stacks plus 18) [[Stone]]
- 480 (7 stacks plus 32) [[Dirt]]
- 327 (5 stacks plus 7) [[Granite]]
- 184 (2 stacks plus 56) [[Hardened Clay|Cyan Hardened Clay]]
- 89 (1 stack plus 25) [[Podzol]]
- 63 [[Seeds]]
- 9 [[Long Grass]]
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Farming Island.|

# Festive Sinker
Categories: Common items, Items, Fishing, Tools
[Festive Sinker]
Type: Rod Part ([[Rod Parts]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Skill Level ([[Skills]]) Requirement: Fishing V (5) ([[Fishing]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FESTIVE_SINKER
Tooltip: Grants a small chance to catch Gifts during the Season of Jerry.; Talk to Roddy in the Backwater Bayou to apply this Sinker to a Fishing Rod.; ❣ Requires Fishing Skill 5.; COMMON ROD PART
A Festive Sinker is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Rod Parts|Rod Part]] that grants a [[White Gift]], [[Green Gift]], or [[Red Gift]] when catching a [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] or item, while [[Jerry's Workshop]] is open.
## Obtaining
A Festive Sinker can be purchased from [[Sherry]] for 50,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Applying a Festive Sinker at [[Roddy]] grants a 5% (1 in 20 catches) chance to get [[White Gift]], [[Green Gift]], or [[Red Gift]] when [[Fishing]] while [[Jerry's Workshop]] is open.
|Item|Weight|Pool Chance|Overall Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[White Gift]]|20|80% (1 in 1.25 catches)|4% (1 in 25 catches)|
|[[Green Gift]]|4|16% (1 in 6.25 catches)|0.8% (1 in 125 catches)|
|[[Red Gift]]|1|4% (1 in 25 catches)|0.2% (1 in 500 catches)|
These items are rolled before any other catch, meaning effect of Festive Sinker will work even if player's [[Sea Creature Chance]] is 100%.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 12, 2025|Added Festive Sinker.|
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Festive Sinker as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
|Aug 25, 2026|Lowered the [[Rarity]] from RARE ([[Rarity]]) to COMMON ([[Rarity]]).|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Mar 18, 2025|0.22|Added Festive Sinker.|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Festive Sinker as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
|Sep 1, 2026|0.27.1|Lowered the [[Rarity]] from RARE ([[Rarity]]) to COMMON ([[Rarity]]).|

[Shop]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FIG_LOG
Tooltip: Collection Item; Each ring tells tales of sun-kissed days. Hold it close, and you might hear its stories.; Right-click to view recipes!; COMMON
Fig Log is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Foraging]] [[Collections|Collection]] Item.
## Collection
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|500|[[Tiny Scaffolding]] Recipe|+4|
|II|1,000|[[Enchanted Fig Log]] Recipe|+4|
|III|2,500|[[Medium Scaffolding]] Recipe|+4|
|IV|5,000|[[Fig Hew]] Recipe|+4|
|V|10,000|[[Large Scaffolding]] Recipe|+4|
|VI|25,000|[[Figstone]] Recipe; [[Figstone Splitter]] Recipe|+4|
|VII|50,000|[[Sparrow Shard]] Recipe|+4|
|VIII|100,000|[[Fig Armor]] Recipe|+4|
|IX|150,000|+100,000 [[Foraging]]Experience; +1 Foraging Level Cap|+4|
## Obtaining
Fig Log is obtained by breaking fig trees in [[West Reaches]], [[South Reaches]], [[North Reaches]], [[Evergreen Plateau]] and [[Tangleburg]] on [[Galatea]]. It has a toughness of 10. It grants base +15 [[Foraging|Foraging XP]].
### Style
Cutting a Fig tree with the correct style allows the player to cut the tree more efficiently. For a Fig tree, the player starts with the trunk and then onto the branches.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Tiny Scaffolding]]|Fig Log I (1)|4x [[Stretching Sticks]] [[Fig Log]] 64x Fig Log|Recipe: Fig Log x16 ×4 + [[Stretching Sticks]] ×4 → [[Tiny Scaffolding]]|
|[[Enchanted Fig Log]]|Fig Log II (2)|[[Fig Log]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Fig Log|Recipe: Fig Log x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Fig Log]]|
## Milestones
Tree milestones are milestones that can be progressed by receiving Tree Gifts. They reward [[Sweep]], [[Foraging]] and [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]].
|Milestone|Tree Gifts|Rewards|Cumulative Rewards|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|10|+1 [[Sweep]]; +2000 [[Foraging]] XP; +1 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|+1 [[Sweep]]; +2000 [[Foraging]] XP; +1 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|II|50|+1 [[Sweep]]; +3000 [[Foraging]] XP; +1 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|+2 [[Sweep]]; +5000 [[Foraging]] XP; +2 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|III|250|+1 [[Sweep]]; +4000 [[Foraging]] XP; +1 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|+3 [[Sweep]]; +9000 [[Foraging]] XP; +3 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|IV|500|+1 [[Sweep]]; +5000 [[Foraging]] XP; +1 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|+4 [[Sweep]]; +14000 [[Foraging]] XP; +4 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|V|1000|+1 [[Sweep]]; +7500 [[Foraging]] XP; +1 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|+5 [[Sweep]]; +21500 [[Foraging]] XP; +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|VI|2500|+1 [[Sweep]]; +10000 [[Foraging]] XP; +1 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|+6 [[Sweep]]; +31500 [[Foraging]] XP; +6 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|VII|5000|+1 [[Sweep]]; +15000 [[Foraging]] XP; +1 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|+7 [[Sweep]]; +46500 [[Foraging]] XP; +7 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
## Fig Collector Attribute
The [[Sparrow Shard]] grants 1-10 [[Fig Fortune]] per every digit in the Fig Log collection, up to 8-80 [[Fig Fortune]] as 10,000,000 is limit to the ability.

# Fire Talisman
Categories: Common items, Accessories, Craftable items, Blaze Rod Collection, Talisman
[Fire Talisman]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Blaze Rod IV (4) ([[Blaze Rod]])
[Item Properties]
Upgradeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 6,480 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 1,440x (22 stacks plus 32 (1,440 in total)) [[Blaze Rod]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FIRE_TALISMAN
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +3; Provides immunity against damage from Fire.; While in the Crimson Isle, grants a 20%; damage reduction instead.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; COMMON ACCESSORY
The Fire Talisman is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that gives full immunity to [[Damage Calculation|Fire Damage]]. On the [[Crimson Isle]], it instead gives 20% less damage. It can be crafted after reaching [[Blaze Rod|Blaze Rod IV (4)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Blaze Rod IV (4) ([[Blaze Rod]])|9x [[Enchanted Blaze Powder]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Blaze Powder]] ×9 → Fire Talisman|
## Usage
The Fire Talisman provides full immunity to [[Damage Calculation|Fire Damage]]. However, this does not work in the [[Trials of Fire]].
This accessory is moderately useful when in the [[Crimson Isle]]. A player may be set on fire by a [[Blaze]] or could be hit by a fireball from a [[Ghast]] or [[Magma Cube]]. It is especially useful for collecting [[Fairy Souls]] in the [[Crimson Isle]].
## History

|Feb 22, 2022|Changed the effect of the Fire Talisman to give 20% reduced Fire Damage ([[Damage Calculation]]) (from 100%) and Full immunity on [[Private Island]]s.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Changed the effect of the Fire Talisman to give 20% reduced Fire Damage ([[Damage Calculation]]) (from 100%) and immunity on [[Private Island]]s.|
|Apr 25, 2022|Changed the effect of the Fire Talisman to give 20% reduced Fire Damage ([[Damage Calculation]]) on [[Crimson Isle]] and immunity on other islands.|

The Firefox Shard is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Torrhus Mastery]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Firefox Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Firefox]] with a [[Lassos|Lasso]].
### Fusion
A Firefox Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Firefox Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Torrhus Mastery]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Torrhus Mastery ([[Attributes]]); Grants +10–100 [[Helix Fortune]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|3|4|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|5|9|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|6|15|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|7|22|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|8|30|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|10|40|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|14|54|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|18|72|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|24|96|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Firefox Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Firefox Shard has the following description:
- Their eyes glow with an intense heat, allowing them to see clearly even on the darkest of nights.

# Fish Bait
Categories: Common items, Items in Sacks, Items, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Fishing
[Fish Bait]
Type: [[Bait]]
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]); Medium Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]); Large Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]])
[Shop]
Sell: 20 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 2x [[Raw Cod]] 1x [[Raw Salmon]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FISH_BAIT
Tooltip: Fishing Bait Consumes on Cast; Grants +45 Fishing Speed.; COMMON BAIT
Fish Bait is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Bait]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Raw Salmon VI (6) ([[Raw Salmon]])|1x [[Raw Salmon]] 2x [[Raw Cod]]|Recipe: [[Raw Salmon]] + [[Raw Cod]] ×2 → Fish Bait|
### Quests
[[Fish Bait]] 16 Fish Bait can be obtained as a one-time reward from [[Angler Angus]].
[[Fish Bait]] 64 (1 stack) Fish Bait is given after completing [[Fisherman Gerald]]'s Finding the Engine task.
### Seal's Treat Bag
128x Fish Bait have a 5.5% (1 in 18) chance to be obtained from a [[Seal's Treat Bag]].
## Usage
### Fishing
Fish Bait can be kept in either the player's inventory or [[Bait Sack]]. When casting a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], a Fish Bait will be consumed to grant +45 [[Fishing Speed]]. Note that the [[Fishing Speed]] doesn't show up in the player's stats. The effects last until the player reels in their rod. If the player doesn't catch anything when reeling, or otherwise cancels their catch, the Bait will be refunded.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]]|Lily Pad VI (6) ([[Lily Pad]])|1x [[Dark Bait]] 1x [[Light Bait]] [[Fish Bait]] 1x Fish Bait|Recipe: Fish Bait + [[Light Bait]] + [[Dark Bait]] → [[Whale Bait]]|

# Fish Food
Categories: Common items, Items
[Fish Food]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FISH_FOOD
Tooltip: Despite its name, can be given to any pet! Feeding this to your pet will not increase its candy count.; Grants 0 Pet EXP.; Right-click on your summoned pet to feed it!; COMMON
Fish Food is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
Fish Food is obtained upon losing a duel with a [[Loudmouth Bass]]. The item Pet XP can be calculated using the following formula:
PetXP=min⁡(1000000,1000*LoserBassWeight)
### Calculator
calcpage  = Calculator:Loudmouth Bass
 template  = Template:Calculator/Loudmouth_Bass
 form      = loudmouthBassCalcForm
 calcname  = Loudmouth Bass Calculator
 result    = inner

 param     = weight|Loser bass mass|0|number
Loading Calculator...
If the calculator fails to load up, please report to an Administrator.
## Usage
Right-clicking with Fish Food in hand will add Pet XP to the player's active Pet. Fish Food doesn't have any limits, unlike [[Candy (disambiguation)|Carrot Candy]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jan 28, 2025|Added Fish Food.|
Linked from: [[Fish (disambiguation)]], [[Loudmouth Bass]], [[Pet Candies]], [[Pets]]

The Fish Hat is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Helmets|Helmet]] unlocked at [[Raw Cod|Raw Cod I (1)]]. As of v0.7.4, it can be used in conjunction with a [[Magical Water Bucket]] and [[Raw Cod]] to turn the [[Melancholic Viking]] into a Joyful Viking, allowing you to access his shop.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Raw Cod I (1) ([[Raw Cod]])|5x [[Raw Cod]]|Recipe: [[Raw Cod]] ×5 → Fish Hat|
## Usage
Helmet:
Fish Hat [[Health]]: +5
[Effects]
[Ability: Splash SNEAK; Crouch to splash some water around.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 10; Cooldown: 1s]
## Trivia
- The model is based on the Pokémon Magikarp. Other items like [[Blessed Bait]] and the [[Flying Fish Pet]] were recolored versions of the hat.
- When the Splash ability is used, a message saying "It's not very effective..." will appear in the chat. This is a reference to Magikarp's iconic useless move, Splash. The message refers to a move hitting a Pokémon with a resisting type.
- This item is part of the [[Melancholic Viking|Melancholic Viking's]] quest.

Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Gives +20% pet exp for Fishing.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; COMMON PET ITEM
A Fishing Exp Boost is a [[Pet Items|Pet Item]].
## Obtaining
- A [[Rarity|COMMON]] Fishing Exp Boost can be purchased from [[Zog]] in the [[Village]] for 60,000 [[Coins]].
- An [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] Fishing Exp Boost has a 0.05% chance to drop from an [[Inkling]].
- A [[Rarity|RARE]] Fishing Exp Boost has a 0.05% chance to drop from a [[Snapping Turtle]].
- An [[Rarity|EPIC]] Fishing Exp Boost has a 0.05% chance to drop from a [[Manta Ray]].
## Usage
|Rarity|Usage|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Right-clicking a Pet ([[Pets]]) whilst holding a Fishing Exp Boost will equip it to the Pet ([[Pets]]), granting it +20% Pet ([[Pets]]) Exp for [[Fishing]]. If another Pet Item ([[Pet Items]]) were equipped on the Pet ([[Pets]]), it would refund the Fishing Exp Boost for 25,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Right-clicking a Pet ([[Pets]]) whilst holding a Fishing Exp Boost will equip it to the Pet ([[Pets]]), granting it +30% Pet ([[Pets]]) Exp for [[Fishing]]. If another Pet Item ([[Pet Items]]) were equipped on the Pet ([[Pets]]), it would refund the Fishing Exp Boost for 50,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Right-clicking a Pet ([[Pets]]) whilst holding a Fishing Exp Boost will equip it to the Pet ([[Pets]]), granting it +40% Pet ([[Pets]]) Exp for [[Fishing]]. If another Pet Item ([[Pet Items]]) were equipped on the Pet ([[Pets]]), it would refund the Fishing Exp Boost for 100,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Right-clicking a Pet ([[Pets]]) whilst holding a Fishing Exp Boost will equip it to the Pet ([[Pets]]), granting it +50% Pet ([[Pets]]) Exp for [[Fishing]]. If another Pet Item ([[Pet Items]]) were equipped on the Pet ([[Pets]]), it would refund the Fishing Exp Boost for 250,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.|

Item ID: LARGE_ENCHANTED_FISHING_SACK
Tooltip: Holds variouis items obtained from Fishing! Items you pickup go directly into your sacks.; Capacity: 640 of each item Multiple sacks sum their capacity!; Right Click to open!; COMMON SACK
The Fishing Sack is a [[Sacks|Sack]] unlocked in the [[Pufferfish]] collection at [[Pufferfish|Pufferfish II (2)]], [[Pufferfish|Pufferfish V (5)]], [[Pufferfish|Pufferfish VII (7)]], [[Pufferfish|Pufferfish IX (9)]], and [[Pufferfish|Pufferfish X (10)]] with increasing capacity. The Small Fishing Sack can hold 2,240 of each item, the Medium Fishing Sack can hold 6,720 of each item, and the Large Fishing Sack can hold 20,160 of each item. Beginner Sack holds 640 items, but only includes vanilla resources. The Large Enchanted Fishing Sack can hold 20,160 of enchanted items.
## Obtaining
### Merchant
A Beginner Fishing Sack can be bought for 250 [[Coins]] at [[Fishing Merchant]] in the [[Hub Island|Hub]] near the [[Fishing Outpost]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Beginner Fishing Sack ([[Fishing Sack]])|Pufferfish II (2) ([[Pufferfish]])|1x [[Leather]] 8x [[Pufferfish]]|Recipe: [[Pufferfish]] ×8 + [[Leather]] → [[Fishing Sack]]|
|Small Fishing Sack ([[Fishing Sack]])|Pufferfish V (5) ([[Pufferfish]])|1x Beginner Fishing Sack ([[Fishing Sack]]) 8x [[Enchanted Pufferfish]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Pufferfish]] ×8 + [[Fishing Sack]] → [[Fishing Sack]]|
|Medium Fishing Sack ([[Fishing Sack]])|Pufferfish VII (7) ([[Pufferfish]])|1x Small Fishing Sack ([[Fishing Sack]]) 32x [[Enchanted Pufferfish]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Pufferfish]] x4 ×8 + [[Fishing Sack]] → [[Fishing Sack]]|
|Large Fishing Sack ([[Fishing Sack]])|Pufferfish IX (9) ([[Pufferfish]])|1x Small Fishing Sack ([[Fishing Sack]]) 64x [[Enchanted Pufferfish]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Pufferfish]] x8 ×8 + [[Fishing Sack]] → [[Fishing Sack]]|
|Large Enchanted Fishing Sack ([[Fishing Sack]])|Pufferfish X (10) ([[Pufferfish]])|12x [[Enchanted Leather]] 16x [[Enchanted String]] 112x (1 stack plus 48 (112 in total)) [[Enchanted Pufferfish]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Pufferfish]] x16 ×7 + [[Enchanted String]] x16 + [[Enchanted Leather]] x12 → [[Fishing Sack]]|
## Usage
The Fishing Sack can store [[Fishing]]-related items.
|Sack|Capacity|Items|
|-|-|-|
|Beginner Fishing Sack ([[Fishing Sack]])|640|[[Clay Ball]], [[Ink Sac]], [[Lily Pad]], [[Prismarine Crystals]], [[Prismarine Shard]], [[Pufferfish]], [[Raw Cod]], [[Raw Salmon]], [[Sponge]], [[Tropical Fish]]|
|Small Fishing Sack ([[Fishing Sack]])|2,240|[[Agarimoo Tongue]], [[Alligator Skin]], [[Blue Ring]], [[Blue Shark Tooth]], [[Broken Radar]], [[Bronze Bowl]], [[Busted Belt Buckle]], [[Can of Worms]], [[Chum]], [[Clay Ball]], [[Emperor's Skull]], [[Frogcoin]], [[Glowing Mushroom]], [[Half-Eaten Mushroom]], [[Ink Sac]], [[Lily Pad]], [[Lotus]], [[Nurse Shark Tooth]], [[Old Leather Boot]], [[Prismarine Crystals]], [[Prismarine Shard]], [[Pufferfish]], [[Raw Cod]], [[Raw Salmon]], [[Rusty Coin]], [[Shark Fin]], [[Sponge]], [[Tiger Shark Tooth]], [[Torn Cloth]], [[Tropical Fish]], [[Worm Membrane]], [[EroC enotS egrofeR]]|
|Medium Fishing Sack ([[Fishing Sack]])|6,720|(same as above)|
|Large Fishing Sack ([[Fishing Sack]])|20,160|(same as above)|
|Large Enchanted Fishing Sack ([[Fishing Sack]])|20,160|[[Condensed Lily Pad]], [[Enchanted Clay Ball]], [[Enchanted Clay Block]], [[Enchanted Cooked Cod]], [[Enchanted Cooked Salmon]], [[Enchanted Ink Sac]], [[Enchanted Lily Pad]], [[Enchanted Prismarine Crystals]], [[Enchanted Prismarine Shard]], [[Enchanted Pufferfish]], [[Enchanted Raw Cod]], [[Enchanted Raw Salmon]], [[Enchanted Shark Fin]], [[Enchanted Sponge]], [[Enchanted Tropical Fish]], [[Enchanted Wet Sponge]], [[Gold Lotus]], [[Silver Lotus]]|

## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Flash Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Rarity|COMMON]] and Combat Category
- Second: [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] or higher
||
||

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Flash Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Light Elemental]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Light Elemental ([[Attributes]]); Grants +1–10 [[Strength]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Gravel II (2) ([[Gravel]])|2x [[Stick]] 10x [[Flint]]|Recipe: [[Flint]] x10 + [[Stick]] ×2 → Flint Shovel|
## Usage
### Tool
Shovel:
Flint Shovel
[Effects]
[Grants a 100% chance of receiving flint from gravel.]
### Minion Upgrade
Flint Shovel can be placed in a [[Gravel Minion]]. It makes the minion always collect [[Flint]].
## Trivia

Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Gives +20% pet exp for Foraging.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; COMMON PET ITEM
A Foraging Exp Boost is a [[Pet Items|Pet Item]].
## Obtaining
### Common
A [[Rarity|COMMON]] Foraging Exp Boost can be purchased from [[Zog]] in the [[Village]] for 60,000 [[Coins]].
### Uncommon
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv60]|[[Ent]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
### Rare

|-|-|-|-|-|

### Epic
