|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv90]|[[Soul of the Alpha]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.01%|
[[Rarity|EPIC]] Foraging Exp Boost also has a 0.025% (1 in 4,000) chance to be obtained from [[Tree Gifts|Helix Tree Gift]].
## Usage
|Rarity|Usage|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Right-clicking a Pet ([[Pets]]) whilst holding a Foraging Exp Boost will equip it to the Pet ([[Pets]]), granting it +20% Pet ([[Pets]]) Exp for [[Foraging]]. If another Pet Item ([[Pet Items]]) were equipped on the Pet ([[Pets]]), it would refund the Foraging Exp Boost for 25,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Right-clicking a Pet ([[Pets]]) whilst holding a Foraging Exp Boost will equip it to the Pet ([[Pets]]), granting it +30% Pet ([[Pets]]) Exp for [[Foraging]]. If another Pet Item ([[Pet Items]]) were equipped on the Pet ([[Pets]]), it would refund the Foraging Exp Boost for 50,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Right-clicking a Pet ([[Pets]]) whilst holding a Foraging Exp Boost will equip it to the Pet ([[Pets]]), granting it +40% Pet ([[Pets]]) Exp for [[Foraging]]. If another Pet Item ([[Pet Items]]) were equipped on the Pet ([[Pets]]), it would refund the Foraging Exp Boost for 100,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Right-clicking a Pet ([[Pets]]) whilst holding a Foraging Exp Boost will equip it to the Pet ([[Pets]]), granting it +50% Pet ([[Pets]]) Exp for [[Foraging]]. If another Pet Item ([[Pet Items]]) were equipped on the Pet ([[Pets]]), it would refund the Foraging Exp Boost for 250,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.|

Tooltip: Holds various items obtained from Foraging! Items you pickup go directly into your sacks.; Capacity: 640 of each item Multiple sacks sum their capacity!; Right Click to open!; COMMON SACK
The Foraging Sack is a [[Sacks|Sack]] unlocked in the [[Birch Log]] collection at [[Birch Log|Birch Log II (2)]], [[Birch Log|Birch Log V (5)]], [[Birch Log|Birch Log VII (7)]], [[Birch Log|Birch Log VIII (8)]], and [[Birch Log|Birch Log IX (9)]] with increasing capacity. The Small Foraging Sack can hold 2,240 of each item, the Medium Foraging Sack can hold 6,720 of each item, and the Large Foraging Sack can hold 20,160 of each item. Beginner Sack holds 640 items, but only includes early game resources. The Large Enchanted Foraging Sack holds 20,160 of enchanted items.
## Obtaining
### Merchant
The Beginner Foraging Sack can be bought for 250 [[Coins]] at [[Lumber Merchant]] in the [[Hub Island|Hub]] near the [[Foraging Camp]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Beginner Foraging Sack ([[Foraging Sack]])|Birch Log II (2) ([[Birch Log]])|1x [[Leather]] 8x [[Birch Log]]|Recipe: [[Birch Log]] ×8 + [[Leather]] → [[Foraging Sack]]|
|Small Foraging Sack ([[Foraging Sack]])|Birch Log V (5) ([[Birch Log]])|1x Beginner Foraging Sack ([[Foraging Sack]]) 8x [[Enchanted Birch Log]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Birch Log]] ×8 + [[Foraging Sack]] → [[Foraging Sack]]|
|Medium Foraging Sack ([[Foraging Sack]])|Birch Log VII (7) ([[Birch Log]])|1x Small Foraging Sack ([[Foraging Sack]]) 32x [[Enchanted Birch Log]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Birch Log]] x4 ×8 + [[Foraging Sack]] → [[Foraging Sack]]|
|Large Foraging Sack ([[Foraging Sack]])|Birch Log VIII (8) ([[Birch Log]])|1x Medium Foraging Sack ([[Foraging Sack]]) 64x [[Enchanted Birch Log]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Birch Log]] x8 ×8 + [[Foraging Sack]] → [[Foraging Sack]]|
|Large Enchanted Foraging Sack ([[Foraging Sack]])|Birch Log IX (9) ([[Birch Log]])|12x [[Enchanted Leather]] 16x [[Enchanted String]] 112x (1 stack plus 48 (112 in total)) [[Enchanted Birch Log]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Birch Log]] x16 ×7 + [[Enchanted String]] x16 + [[Enchanted Leather]] x12 → [[Foraging Sack]]|
## Usage
The Foraging Sack can store [[Foraging]]-related items.
|Sack|Capacity|Items|
|-|-|-|
|Beginner Foraging Sack ([[Foraging Sack]])|640|[[Acacia Log]], [[Birch Log]], [[Dark Oak Log]], [[Jungle Log]], [[Oak Log]], [[Spruce Log]]|
|Small Foraging Sack ([[Foraging Sack]])|2,240|[[Acacia Log]], Acacia Sapling ([[Saplings]]), [[Agatha's Prize]], [[Apple]], [[Bamboo]], [[Birch Log]], Birch Sapling ([[Saplings]]), [[Dark Oak Log]], Dark Oak Sapling ([[Saplings]]), [[Deep Root]], [[Fig Log]], [[Flexbone]], [[Gill Membrane]], [[Helix Log]], [[Honeycomb]], [[Jungle Log]], Jungle Sapling ([[Saplings]]), [[Lushlilac]], [[Mangrove Log]], [[Miria's Prize]], [[Oak Log]], Oak Sapling ([[Saplings]]), [[Sea Lumies]], [[Spruce Log]], Spruce Sapling ([[Saplings]]), [[Stick]], [[Stretching Sticks]], [[Sturdy Bone]], [[Tender Wood]], [[Vinesap]], [[Wet Water]]|
|Medium Foraging Sack ([[Foraging Sack]])|6,720|(same as above)|
|Large Foraging Sack ([[Foraging Sack]])|20,160|(same as above)|
|Large Enchanted Foraging Sack ([[Foraging Sack]])|20,160|[[Enchanted Acacia Log]], [[Enchanted Birch Log]], [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]], [[Enchanted Fig Log]], [[Enchanted Helix Log]], [[Enchanted Honeycomb]], [[Enchanted Honeycomb Block]], [[Enchanted Jungle Log]], [[Enchanted Mangrove Log]], [[Enchanted Oak Log]], [[Enchanted Ruby Veilshroom]], [[Enchanted Sea Lumies]], [[Enchanted Spruce Log]], [[Enchanted Tender Wood]], [[Enchanted Vinesap]], [[Figstone]], [[Helixis]], [[Mangcore]]|

The Foxtrot Shard is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Foraging Speed]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Foxtrot Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Foxtrot]] with a [[Critter Capsule]] in the [[Critter Safari]].
### Fusion
A Foxtrot Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Foxtrot Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Foraging Speed]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Foraging Speed ([[Attributes]]); Grants +3–30 [[Speed]] while on Foraging Islands.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|3|4|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|5|9|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|6|15|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|7|22|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|8|30|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|10|40|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|14|54|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|18|72|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|24|96|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Foxtrot Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Foxtrot Shard has the following description:
- The Foxtrot moves with unparalleled elegance, almost gliding across the forest floor.

Tooltip: Collection Item; An unnatural, nigh-unbreakable material. Of those unlucky few who have discovered where it originates, none have lived to tell the tale.; COMMON DWARVEN METAL
Glacite, pronounced [ɡlˈeɪsˈaɪt], is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] resource in the [[Mining]] Collection that can be obtained in the [[Glacite Tunnels]] and [[Glacite Mineshafts]] by mining Glacite veins.
## Collection
Collecting Glacite increases the player's Glacite [[Collections|collection]], which grants useful items and perks upon collecting certain amounts of Glacite.
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|100|+1 [[Mining Fortune]]|+4|
|II|1,000|[[Enchanted Glacite]] Recipe; [[Cold Resistance Potion]] Recipe|+4|
|III|5,000|[[Glacite Amalgamation]] Dwarven Forge Recipe|+4|
|IV|10,000|[[Glacite Golem Pet]] Recipe|+4|
|V|25,000|[[Frigid Husk]] Dwarven Forge Recipe|+4|
|VI|50,000|+1 [[Mining Fortune]]|+4|
|VII|100,000|+1 [[Mining Fortune]]|+4|
|VIII|250,000|+1 [[Mining Fortune]]|+4|
|IX|500,000|+10,000 [[Mining]]Experience|+4|
## Obtaining
### Mining
Glacite is obtained by mining [[Packed Ice]] in the [[Glacite Tunnels]] or [[Glacite Mineshafts]]. It requires 9 [[Breaking Power]] to mine.

# Gloomgourd
Categories: Common items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients, Mutations
[Gloomgourd]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GLOOMGOURD
Tooltip: Heavy and strange, it seems to carry a hidden weight.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; COMMON MUTATION
A Gloomgourd is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Gloomgourd can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there is 1 [[Pumpkin]] and 1 [[Melon]] within 1 block of farmland.
When placed near another crop, Gloomgourd retains watering status by 50% and allows for additional items to be found.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Gloomgourds also drop 34 [[Pumpkin]], 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Melon Slice]], and +320 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Gloomgourd can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 10,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 5 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Gloomgourd grants an additional 250 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Gloomgourds are used to create a [[Chocoberry]] and [[Soggybud]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Turbo Gourd]]|None|2x [[Aggourdian]] [[Gloomgourd]] 2x Gloomgourd 2x [[Polished Pumpkin]] 2x [[Squash]]|Recipe: [[Aggourdian]] x2 + Gloomgourd x2 + [[Squash]] x2 + [[Polished Pumpkin]] x2 → [[Turbo Gourd]]|
## Gallery
- Gloomgourd in the Greenhouse Gloomgourd in the [[Greenhouse]]

|Dec 5, 2025|Added Gloomgourd.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

The Golden Ghoul Shard is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Midas Touch]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Golden Ghoul Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Golden Ghoul]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion
A Golden Ghoul Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Golden Ghoul Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Midas Touch]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Midas Touch ([[Attributes]]); Grants +1–10 [[Coins]] per kill.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|3|4|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|5|9|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|6|15|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|7|22|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|8|30|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|10|40|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|14|54|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|18|72|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|24|96|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Golden Ghoul Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||

[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Golden Ghoul Shard has the following description:
- These undead creatures are driven by an insatiable greed, forever seeking out precious metals and jewels.

Griffin Upgrade Stones are items crafted from various materials obtained during the [[Mythological Ritual]] and used to upgrade the [[Griffin Pet]]'s rarity at [[Kat]].
The Base Griffin Upgrade Stone is a base component of each upgrade stone.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Base Griffin Upgrade Stone ([[Griffin Upgrade Stone]])|None|8x [[Griffin Feather]] 8x [[Enchanted Cobblestone]] 16x [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Griffin Feather]] x2 ×4 + [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] x4 ×4 + [[Enchanted Cobblestone]] x8 → Base Griffin Upgrade Stone|
|UNCOMMON Griffin Upgrade Stone ([[Griffin Upgrade Stone]])|None|1x Base Griffin Upgrade Stone ([[Griffin Upgrade Stone]]) 8x [[Griffin Feather]] 32x [[Ancient Claw]] 64x [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Griffin Feather]] x4 ×2 + [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] x16 ×4 + [[Ancient Claw]] x16 ×2 + Base Griffin Upgrade Stone → [[Griffin Upgrade Stone]]|
|RARE Griffin Upgrade Stone ([[Griffin Upgrade Stone]])|None|1x Base Griffin Upgrade Stone ([[Griffin Upgrade Stone]]) 4x [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 16x [[Griffin Feather]] 64x [[Ancient Claw]]|Recipe: [[Griffin Feather]] x8 ×2 + [[Enchanted Gold Block]] ×4 + [[Ancient Claw]] x32 ×2 + Base Griffin Upgrade Stone → [[Griffin Upgrade Stone]]|
|EPIC Griffin Upgrade Stone ([[Griffin Upgrade Stone]])|None|1x Base Griffin Upgrade Stone ([[Griffin Upgrade Stone]]) 4x [[Mythos Fragment]] 4x [[Enchanted Ancient Claw]] 12x [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 64x [[Griffin Feather]]|Recipe: [[Griffin Feather]] x32 ×2 + [[Mythos Fragment]] x4 + [[Enchanted Ancient Claw]] x2 ×2 + Base Griffin Upgrade Stone + [[Enchanted Gold Block]] x4 ×3 → [[Griffin Upgrade Stone]]|
|LEGENDARY Griffin Upgrade Stone ([[Griffin Upgrade Stone]])|None|1x [[Braided Griffin Feather]] 1x Base Griffin Upgrade Stone ([[Griffin Upgrade Stone]]) 16x [[Mythos Fragment]] 32x [[Enchanted Ancient Claw]] 96x (1 stack plus 32 (96 in total)) [[Enchanted Gold Block]]|Recipe: [[Mythos Fragment]] x8 ×2 + [[Braided Griffin Feather]] + [[Enchanted Ancient Claw]] x16 ×2 + Base Griffin Upgrade Stone + [[Enchanted Gold Block]] x32 ×3 → [[Griffin Upgrade Stone]]|
|MYTHIC Griffin Upgrade Stone ([[Griffin Upgrade Stone]])|None|1x Base Griffin Upgrade Stone ([[Griffin Upgrade Stone]]) 2x [[Braided Griffin Feather]] 48x [[Mythos Fragment]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Ancient Claw]] 192x (3 stacks (192 items total)) [[Enchanted Gold Block]]|Recipe: [[Braided Griffin Feather]] ×2 + [[Mythos Fragment]] x48 + [[Enchanted Ancient Claw]] x64 ×2 + Base Griffin Upgrade Stone + [[Enchanted Gold Block]] x64 ×3 → [[Griffin Upgrade Stone]]|
## Usage
Griffin Upgrade Stones can be used at [[Kat]] to upgrade the Griffin Pet from the directly preceding rarity.
## Trivia
- Griffin Upgrade Stones cannot be [[Recombobulator 3000|recombobulated]]. If one is already recombobulated, it will be forcibly reverted to its base rarity.
  - Previously, people would scam others by applying a [[Recombobulator 3000]] on a Griffin Upgrade Stone: an [[Griffin Upgrade Stone|Epic Griffin Upgrade Stone]] that was recombobulated to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] would not upgrade a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Griffin Pet]] to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]].

### Fusion

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Groundhog Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Diurnal Animal]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Diurnal Animal ([[Attributes]]); Grants +1–10 [[Sweep]] during the day.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

# Handy Blood Chalice
Aliases: Blood Chalice, Blood chalice
Categories: Common items, Accessories, Slayer, Enderman Slayer, Mob Drops
[Handy Blood Chalice]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Drop Chance: 9Pray RNGesus ([[Drop Chance]])
Slayer Level ([[Slayer]]) Requirement: Enderman Slayer V (5) ([[Enderman Slayer]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HANDY_BLOOD_CHALICE
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +3; Costs 1⸎ Soulflow per 5s in combat:; ⁍ Gain +20 Ferocity; Enabled: On Right-click to toggle!; ☠ Requires Enderman Slayer 5.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; COMMON ACCESSORY
The Handy Blood Chalice is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]]. It increases [[Ferocity]] at a [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]] cost.
## Obtaining
A Handy Blood Chalice has a [[Drop Chance|9Pray RNGesus]] chance to drop from [[Voidgloom Seraph]] Tier IV if the player has [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer LVL V (5)]]. It has a 0.18% (1/566.76) chance to drop.
## Usage
Handy Blood Chalice requires [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer LVL V (5)]] to use.
Its ability can be toggled on and off by holding and right clicking it.
When in combat with it enabled and with sufficient [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]], it increases its user's [[Ferocity]] by 20 and takes away 1 [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]] per 5 seconds.
### Salvaging
Handy Blood Chalice can be salvaged for 15 [[Dragon Essence]].
## Trivia
- It used to have the same texture as [[Cup of Blood]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|May 31, 2021|0.11.5|Added Handy Blood Chalice.|
|Aug 17, 2021|Fixed the item ability working regardless of if the user had the required Slayer level.|
|Feb 6, 2024|0.19.11|Handy Blood Chalice can now be salvaged for 15 [[Dragon Essence]].|
|Feb 22, 2024|Fixed item requirements only affecting whether or not Handy Blood Chalice can be obtained as a drop.|

# Hard Glass
Categories: Common items, Blocks, Craftable items, Sand Collection
[Hard Glass]
Type: Building Block
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 20 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) [[Sand]] 8x [[Coal]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HARD_GLASS
Tooltip: Special hardened glass which doesn't break to explosions.; COMMON
Hard Glass is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] block. It has the property of being very resistant to explosions, making it useful for [[Creeper Minion|Creeper]] and [[Ghast Minion|Ghast Minions]]. It is required to sneak to place on it.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Sand VII (7) ([[Sand]])|1x [[Enchanted Sand]] 8x [[Coal]]|Recipe: [[Coal]] ×8 + [[Enchanted Sand]] → Hard Glass x8|
## Bugs
Hard Glass was used for a bug where you could place [[Minions|Minion Heads]] and other skulls on the block. This has been patched.
Currently, Hard Glass registers as a tile entity.
## Trivia
- This block is similar to Blast-proof Glass, an item in Bed Wars, a separate Hypixel minigame. However, it does not protect blocks around it that are not exposed from explosions.
  - Another item that originates from Bed Wars is the [[Bridge Egg]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 9, 2019|0.6|Added Hard Glass.|
|Apr 30, 2021|Minion heads can no longer be placed directly on Hard Glass.|

A condensed form of rock only found at extreme depths.
Hard Stone is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] Block found in the [[Crystal Hollows]], [[Glacite Tunnels]], and [[Glacite Mineshafts]].
## Collection
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|50|[[Hard Stone Minion]] Recipe|+4|
|II|1,000|[[Heat Armor]] Recipe|+4|
|III|5,000|[[Enchanted Hard Stone]] Recipe|+4|
|IV|50,000|[[Scorched Topaz]] Dwarven Forge Recipe|+4|
|V|150,000|[[Flamebreaker Armor]] Recipe|+4|
|VI|300,000|[[Concentrated Stone]] Recipe|+4|
|VII|1,000,000|[[Silex]] Recipe|+4|
Note: [[Yog Armor]] is also unlocked at Hard Stone V (5) (5), but it isn't listed.
## Obtaining
Hard Stone can be obtained by mining [[Stone]] blocks in the [[Crystal Hollows]], [[Glacite Tunnels]], or [[Glacite Mineshafts]] (or or [[Hardened Clay|Cyan Hardened Clay]] blocks in the [[Crystal Hollows]]) using a [[Pickaxes|Pickaxe]], [[Gauntlet]], or [[Drills|Drill]] with a [[Breaking Power]] of at least 4.
The [[Hardened Clay|Cyan Hardened Clay]] variant only appears in the [[Crystal Hollows]]. In other areas, it is [[Mithril]] instead.

- The Strong Arm perk from [[Heart of the Mountain|HotM Tier 8]] grants 25-2500 [[Mining Speed]] on [[Hard Stone]] Hard Stone if the player is not on solid ground. This is a hidden buff.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HELIX_LOG
Tooltip: Collection Item; A particularly resilient type of log that, should they not know the secret to cutting it, will outlast most foragers.; Right-click to view recipes!; COMMON
Helix Log is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Foraging]] [[Collections|Collection]] item obtained from Helix Trees in the [[Torrhus Canyon]].
## Collection
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|500|[[Travel Scroll to Torrhus Canyon]] Recipe|+4|
|II|1,000|[[Enchanted Helix Log]] Recipe|+4|
|III|2,500|[[Lumberjack Talisman]] Recipe|+4|
|IV|5,000|Portal to the Torrhus Canyon ([[Portals]]) Recipe|+4|
|V|10,000|[[Lumberjack Ring]] Recipe|+4|
|VI|25,000|[[Helixis]] Recipe; [[Helix Chopper]] Recipe|+4|
|VII|50,000|[[Hawk Shard]] Recipe|+4|
|VIII|100,000|[[Lumberjack Artifact]] Recipe; [[Helix Armor]] Recipe|+4|
|IX|150,000|+150,000 [[Foraging]]Experience; +1 Foraging Level Cap|+4|
## Obtaining
### Foraging
Helix Logs are obtained by breaking Helix Trees in the [[Torrhus Canyon]] on [[Galatea]]. It has a toughness of 150 [[Toughness]] .
Cutting a Helix tree with the correct style allows the player to cut the tree more efficiently. For a Helix tree, the player starts with the Beige logs ( Stripped Birch Wood) and then the Red logs ( Stripped Mangrove Wood).

|III|100|+3 [[Sweep]]; +15,000 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]]); +4 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|+9 [[Sweep]]; +29,000 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]]); +12 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|IV|250|+3 [[Sweep]]; +20,000 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]]); +4 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|+12 [[Sweep]]; +49,000 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]]); +16 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|V|500|+3 [[Sweep]]; +35,000 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]]); +4 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|+15 [[Sweep]]; +84,000 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]]); +20 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

|VII|2500|+3 [[Sweep]]; +150,000 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]]); +4 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|+21 [[Sweep]]; +309,000 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]]); +28 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
## Helix Collector Attribute
The [[Hawk Shard]] grants 1-10 [[Helix Fortune]] per every digit in the Helix Log collection, up to 8-80 [[Helix Fortune]] as 10,000,000 is limit to the ability.

The Hideongift Shard is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Happy Box]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Hideongift Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: Shulker Family
- Second: [[Rarity|COMMON]] or higher
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Hideongift Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Happy Box]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Happy Box ([[Attributes]]); Grants a +2%–20% chance to obtain an extra Winter Gift ([[Gifts]]) when finding one.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|3|4|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|5|9|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|6|15|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|7|22|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|8|30|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|10|40|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|14|54|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|18|72|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|24|96|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Hideongift Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Hideongift Shard has the following description:
- Hideongift disguises itself as a festive present, its colorful shell mimicking the wrapping of a gift box.

The Hideonsun Shard is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Gold Box]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Hideonsun Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Hideonsun]].
### Fusion
A Hideonsun Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Hideonsun Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Gold Box]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Gold Box ([[Attributes]]); Grants +5–50 [[Ore Fortune]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|3|4|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|5|9|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|6|15|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|7|22|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|8|30|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|10|40|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|14|54|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|18|72|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|24|96|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

A Hideonsun Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Hideonsun Shard has the following description:
- Hideonsun's bullets reflect the blinding light of the sun, making them harder to dodge.

# Hot Dog
Categories: Common items, Items
[Hot Dog]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No

Auctionable: ✕ No

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HOT_DOG
Tooltip: Deposit on Joey's plate.; * Soulbound * COMMON
The Hot Dog is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
Hot Dogs can be obtained from [[Chef]]'s Hot Dog eating competition in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Usage
The player can give [[Hot Dog]] 50 Hot Dogs to [[Joey McPizza]] as part of the Hot Dog eating competition quest. Upon completion, the player is awarded with an [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]].
## Trivia
- Hot Dogs are not stackable, making it harder to complete the quest.
  - It is generally recommended to have at least two players work together.
## History

The Husbandry Sack is a [[Sacks|Sack]] unlocked in the [[Raw Mutton]] collection at [[Raw Mutton|Raw Mutton II (2)]], [[Raw Mutton|Raw Mutton V (5)]], [[Raw Mutton|Raw Mutton VII (7)]], [[Raw Mutton|Raw Mutton IX (9)]], and [[Raw Mutton|Raw Mutton X (10)]] with increasing capacity. The Beginner Sack can hold 640 of each item, the Small Sack can hold 2,240 of each item, the Medium Sack can hold 6,720 of each item, and the Large Sack can hold 20,160 of each item. Large Enchanted Sack can hold 20,160 of enchanted item.
## Obtaining
### Merchant
A Beginner Husbandry Sack can be bought for 250 [[Coins]] at [[Husbandry Merchant]] in the [[The Barn|Barn]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Beginner Husbandry Sack ([[Husbandry Sack]])|Raw Mutton II (2) ([[Raw Mutton]])|1x [[Leather]] 8x [[Raw Mutton]]|Recipe: [[Raw Mutton]] ×8 + [[Leather]] → [[Husbandry Sack]]|
|Small Husbandry Sack ([[Husbandry Sack]])|Raw Mutton V (5) ([[Raw Mutton]])|1x Beginner Husbandry Sack ([[Husbandry Sack]]) 8x [[Enchanted Raw Mutton]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Raw Mutton]] ×8 + [[Husbandry Sack]] → [[Husbandry Sack]]|
|Medium Husbandry Sack ([[Husbandry Sack]])|Raw Mutton VII (7) ([[Raw Mutton]])|1x Small Husbandry Sack ([[Husbandry Sack]]) 32x [[Enchanted Raw Mutton]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Raw Mutton]] x4 ×8 + [[Husbandry Sack]] → [[Husbandry Sack]]|

Dispenses 150; water per second.; Ability: Fill RIGHT CLICK When aiming at a water source, fills your can back up with water.; Water: 0\ 3k; COMMON WATERING CAN
A HydroCan™ 1000 is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Watering Cans|Watering Can]].
## Obtaining
A HydroCan™ 1000 can be claimed from [[Sam]] in [[The Garden]] as part of the quest to unlock the [[Greenhouse]]. It can also be purchased from [[The Garden|SkyMart]] for 5,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
A HydroCan™ 1000 can be used to water crops in the [[Greenhouse]]. When used, it waters crops at a rate of 150 water per second. It has a water capacity of 3,000 and can be refilled by right-clicking [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]].

# Inflatable Jerry
Categories: Common items, Items in Sacks, Items, Catacombs, Dungeons, Tools, Other Tools, Brewing ingredients, Jerry, Dungeon Items
May it be a light to you in dark places, when all other lights go out.
[Inflatable Jerry]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: May it be a light to you in dark places, when all other lights go out.
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Dungeon Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 500 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: INFLATABLE_JERRY
Tooltip: Brewing Ingredient; May it be a light to you in dark places, when all other lights go out.; Dungeons only!; COMMON DUNGEON ITEM
Inflatable Jerry is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Dungeon Items|Dungeon Item]] which can be used to scare away [[Catacombs Mobs|Non-Boss mobs]] in [[Dungeons]]. The mobs will run in the opposite direction, while text displays above their head that reads: "JERRY, RUN"
There is a 20% chance that it will pop when used and not activate.
There are some limitations to the effect. [[Catacombs Mobs|Bosses]] and [[Catacombs Mobs|Mini-Bosses]] are not affected by Jerry. Furthermore, it will always fail to activate around Bosses no matter how many are placed. Thus, in boss fights like [[Necron]], it is advised to stay away from the boss to activate it.
## Obtaining
Inflatable Jerries can be found in [[Dungeons]] while doing [[Dungeon Secrets|Secrets]].
## Trivia
- Its description is quoted from the movie The Fellowship of the Ring.
  - This quote comes from a scene where the Character Galadriel presents Frodo with a phial of light called the Phial of Galadriel.
- Randomly, this item may turn into a [[Spawn Egg|Villager Spawn Egg]].
- The text "JERRY, RUN" is a reference to the movie "Forrest Gump" in where there is a scene where a character says “RUN FORREST, RUN!”.
- Inflatable Jerry can be used as a [[Dungeon Secrets|Dungeon Secret]] for the [[Secret I Potion]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Inflatable Jerry as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 7, 2020|0.8|Added Inflatable Jerry.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Inflatable Jerry to the [[Bazaar]].|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Inflatable Jerry as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

## Obtaining
### Hunting
An Inkling Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] an [[Inkling]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion
An Inkling Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Inkling Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Fisherman]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Fisherman ([[Attributes]]); Has a +1%–10% chance not to consume any bait.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

|Ⅲ (3)|5|9|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

|Ⅶ (7)|10|40|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|14|54|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|18|72|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|24|96|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
An Inkling Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Inkling Shard has the following description:
- A silent guardian of the sky, its vast wings are a tranquil spectacle, often seen soaring high above with a serene grace.
## Bait Saving Items
- [[Spiked Atrocity]]: 10%
- [[Inkling Shard]] Inkling Shard: 10%
- [[Stingy Sinker]]: 10%
Total: 30% chance to not consume [[Bait|Fishing Bait]].

A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Increases the pet's  Crit Damage by 40% and  Crit Chance by 40%.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; COMMON PET ITEM
Iron Claws is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]]. It increased the [[Stats]] of [[Pets]] by +40% [[Crit Damage]] and +40% [[Crit Chance]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
Iron Claws can be purchased from [[Zog]] in the [[Village]] for 750,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Iron Claws will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it +40% [[Crit Damage]] and +40% [[Crit Chance]]. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Iron Claws for 25,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Gold Claws]]|None|[[Iron Claws]] 1x Iron Claws 1x [[Zog Anvil]] 18x [[Enchanted Gold Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Gold Block]] x6 ×3 + Iron Claws + [[Zog Anvil]] → [[Gold Claws]]|
## Tips
- Iron Claws increases a pet's stats by 40% of its existing [[Crit Damage]] and [[Crit Chance]], rather than a flat increase like the [[Big Teeth]] and [[Sharpened Claws]] [[Pet Items]].

Item ID: KALHUIKI_MASK
Tooltip: Health: +100; Defense: +50; A ceremonial mask worn by the members of the Kalhuiki Tribe.; While wearing this mask, members of the Kalhuiki Tribe will not attack you!; This item can be reforged!; COMMON HELMET
The Kalhuiki Mask is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Helmets|Helmet]].
## Obtaining
A Kalhuiki Mask can be purchased from [[Odawa]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]] for 20 [[Planks|Oak Planks]] and 20 [[Sludge Juice]].
## Usage
Helmet:
Kalhuiki Mask [[Health]]: +100 [[Defense]]: +50
Whilst wearing a Kalhuiki Mask, [[Kalhuiki Tribe Member|Kalhuiki Tribe Members]] and [[Kalhuiki Elder|Kalhuiki Elders]] will not attack the player.
## Trivia
If the player has already attacked a [[Kalhuiki Tribe Member]] or [[Kalhuiki Elder]] and puts the Kalhuiki Mask on, the Kalhuiki Tribe Member or Kalhuiki Elders will stop their aggressive behavior until the player removes the mask, in which they will resume their behavior.

# King Talisman
Categories: Common items, Accessories, Dwarven Mines, Talisman
[King Talisman]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

[Shop]
Sell: 2,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: KING_TALISMAN
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +3; Commissions grant +10 HOTM Exp.; * Soulbound * Works while in Accessory Bag!; COMMON ACCESSORY
The King Talisman is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that grants +10 [[Heart of the Mountain]] Exp from [[Commissions]].
## Obtaining
### Kings
The King Talisman is obtained after speaking to all seven [[King|Kings]] while they are the active King. The active King changes every [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Day]]. Every time the player speaks to a unique king, a progress number will appear in the chat. Once all seven kings have been spoken to, the talisman will automatically appear in the player's inventory, and the player will recieve the [[Achievements|"A Royal Meeting" Achievement]].
### Shop Purchase
Once unlocked from the [[King|Kings]], the player can buy more King Talismans from [[Rusty]] for 500,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Accessory:
King Talisman
[Effects]
[Commissions grant +10 HOTM Exp.]

KnockOff™ Cola is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Brews|Brew]].
## Obtaining
The player can purchase a KnockOff™ Cola for 1,500 [[Coins]] from the [[Bartender]].
## Usage
KnockOff™ Cola buffs the [[Strength]] value of [[Potions]] with that stat by +5%. It can be used in place of an [[Awkward Potion]] in a [[Brewing Stand]] to take effect.

Tooltip: Experience I Grants +10% more experience orbs from killing mobs.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; COMMON COMBAT SHARD (ID C9)
The Lapis Zombie Shard is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Experience]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Lapis Zombie Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Lapis Zombie]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Huntraps
A Lapis Zombie Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Lapis Quarry]].
### Fusion
A Lapis Zombie Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Lapis Zombie Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Experience]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Experience ([[Attributes]]); Grants +10%–100% more experience orbs from killing mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|3|4|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|5|9|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|6|15|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|7|22|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|8|30|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|10|40|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|14|54|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|18|72|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|24|96|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Lapis Zombie Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Lapis Zombie Shard has the following description:
- These undead creatures are drawn to the energy of the lapis, their eerie blue glow illuminating the dark caverns.
